H-L\n            col_letter = get_column_letter(col)\n            addr = f\"{col_letter}{r}\"\n            # INDEX over Data H..L rows 21..38\n            formula = (\n                f\"=INDEX(Data!$H${DATA_START_ROW}:$L${DATA_END_ROW},\"\n                f\" MATCH($D{r}, Data!$D${DATA_START_ROW}:$D${DATA_END_ROW}, 0),\"\n                f\" MATCH({col_letter}${YEAR_ROW_TASK}, Data!$H${header_row}:$L${header_row}, 0))\"\n            )\n            task[addr].value = formula\n            v = lookup.get((code, year))\n            if v is None:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"Lookup missing for code={code}, year={year} needed by {addr}\")\n            formula_values[addr] = float(v)\n\n# --- Step 2: net renewable balance formulas + computed values ---\nfor r in range(35, 41):  # compute stats\n    min_v = min(net_vals)\n    max_v = max(net_vals)\n    # median for even n\n    n = len(net_vals_sorted)\n    median_v = (net_vals_sorted[n//2 - 1] + net_vals_sorted[n//2]) / 2.0\n    avg_v = sum(net_vals) / n\n    p25_v = percentile_inc(net_vals, 0.25)\n    p75_v = percentile_inc(net_vals, 0.75)\n\n    computed = {\n        42: min_v,\n        43: max_v,\n        44: median_v,\n        45: avg_v,\n        46: p25_v,\n        47: p75_v,\n    }\n\n    for r, (fn, k) in STAT_ROWS.items():\n        addr = f\"{col_letter}{r}\"\n        if fn in ('MIN', 'MAX', 'MEDIAN', 'AVERAGE'):\n            formula = f\"={fn}({net_range})\"\n        else:\n            formula = f\"={fn}({net_range},{k})\"\n        task[addr].value = formula\n        formula_values[addr] = float(computed[r])\n\n# --- Step 3: weighted mean row 50 ---\nfor ci, year in enumerate(YEARS):\n    col = 8 + ci\n    col_letter = get_column_letter(col)\n    net_rng = f\"{col_letter}$35:{col_letter}$40\"\n    w_rng = f\"{col_letter}$26:{col_letter}$31\"\n    addr = f\"{col_letter}50\"\n    task[addr].value = f\"=SUMPRODUCT({net_rng},{w_rng})/SUM({w_rng})\"\n\n    nets = [formula_values[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    weights = [formula_values[f\"{col_letter}{r}\"] for r in range(26, 32)]\n    num = sum(n*w for n, w in zip(nets, weights))\n    den = sum(weights)\n    formula_values[addr] = num / den if den else 0.0\n\n# Save workbook with formulas\nwb.save(OUTPUT)\n\n# --- Inject cached values into Task sheet XML ---\nSSML_NS = 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main'\nREL_NS = 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships'\nET.register_namespace('', SSML_NS)\n\n# Map sheet name -> worksheet xml target\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT, 'r') as z:\n    workbook_xml = ET.fromstring(z.read('xl/workbook.xml'))\n    rels_xml = ET.fromstring(z.read('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels'))\n\n    # r:id -> target\n    rid_to_target = {}\n    for rel in rels_xml.findall(f'{{{REL_NS}}}Relationship'):\n        rid_to_target[rel.attrib['Id']] = rel.attrib['Target']\n\n    task_target = None\n    sheets_el = workbook_xml.find(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}sheets')\n    for sheet in sheets_el.findall(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}sheet'):\n        name = sheet.attrib.get('name')\n        if name == 'Task':\n            rid = sheet.attrib.get(f'{{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships}}id')\n            task_target = rid_to_target.get(rid)\n            break\n\n    if not task_target:\n        raise RuntimeError('Could not map Task sheet to worksheet XML target')\n\n    task_xml_path = 'xl/' + task_target.lstrip('/')\n    task_sheet_xml_bytes = z.read(task_xml_path)\n\n# Parse and modify Task sheet xml\nroot = ET.fromstring(task_sheet_xml_bytes)\n\n# Build a dict for quick lookup and keep only addresses we care about\n# (includes lookup blocks, net block, stats, weighted mean)\naddr_set = set(formula_values.keys())\n\n# Iterate all cells and update those in addr_set\n# Note: cells are nested under sheetData/row/c\nsheetData = root.find(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}sheetData')\nif sheetData is None:\n    raise RuntimeError('Task sheet XML missing sheetData')\n\nupdated = 0\nmissing_cells = []\n\n# Build index of cells by ref for fast update\ncell_index = {}\nfor row_el in sheetData.findall(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}row'):\n    for c_el in row_el.findall(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}c'):\n        r = c_el.attrib.get('r')\n        if r:\n            cell_index[r] = c_el\n\nfor addr, val in formula_values.items():\n    c_el = cell_index.get(addr)\n    if c_el is None:\n        # Cell might not exist if blank and formula not written? but we wrote formulas via openpyxl\n        missing_cells.append(addr)\n        continue\n\n    # Ensure numeric type\n    if 't' in c_el.attrib:\n        # Remove explicit string typing if any\n        c_el.attrib.pop('t', None)\n\n    # Find formula element\n    f_el = c_el.find(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}f')\n    if f_el is None:\n        # if no formula element, still write value\n        pass\n\n    v_el = c_el.find(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}v')\n    if v_el is None:\n        v_el = ET.Element(f'{{{SSML_NS}}}v')\n        # insert after f if exists, else append\n        if f_el is not None:\n            # insert right after f_el\n            children = list(c_el)\n            try:\n                idx = children.index(f_el)\n                c_el.insert(idx + 1, v_el)\n            except ValueError:\n                c_el.append(v_el)\n        else:\n            c_el.append(v_el)\n\n    # Represent the number; avoid scientific for modest magnitude\n    if val is None or (isinstance(val, float) and (math.isnan(val) or math.isinf(val))):\n        v_el.text = ''\n    else:\n        v_el.text = format(float(val), '.15g')\n    updated += 1\n\nif missing_cells:\n    raise RuntimeError(f'Missing {len(missing_cells)} cell elements in XML (unexpected): {missing_cells[:10]}')\n\nnew_task_xml = ET.tostring(root, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n# Write out modified zip\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(OUTPUT, 'r') as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(TMP_ZIP_OUT, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n    for item in zin.infolist():\n        data_bytes = zin.read(item.filename)\n        if item.filename == task_xml_path:\n            data_bytes = new_task_xml\n        zout.writestr(item, data_bytes)\n\nshutil.move(TMP_ZIP_OUT, OUTPUT)\n\n# --- Validation: read cached values (data_only=True) ---\nwb2 = openpyxl.load_workbook(OUTPUT, data_only=True)\ntask2 = wb2['Task']\n\nranges = [\n    ('H12:L17', 12, 17),\n    ('H19:L24', 19, 24),\n    ('H26:L31', 26, 31),\n    ('H35:L40', 35, 40),\n    ('H42:L47', 42, 47),\n    ('H50:L50', 50, 50),\n]\n\ndef dump_block(r1, r2, c1=8, c2=12):\n    out=[]\n    for r in range(r1, r2+1):\n        row=[]\n        for c in range(c1, c2+1):\n            row.append(task2.cell(r,c).value)\n        out.append(row)\n    return out\n\nfor label, r1, r2 in ranges:\n    block = dump_block(r1, r2)\n    # check non-None\n    none_ct = sum(1 for row in block for v in row if v is None)\n    print(label, 'none=', none_ct, 'sample=', block[0][:2] if block else None)\n\n# spot-check one campus-year calc: Cedar 2018 net = (ren-grid)/base*100\nced_ren = task2['H12'].value\nced_grid = task2['H19'].value\nced_base = task2['H26'].value\nced_net = task2['H35'].value\nprint('Spot check Cedar 2018:', ced_ren, ced_grid, ced_base, 'net=', ced_net, 'expected=', (ced_ren-ced_grid)/ced_base*100)\n\nprint('Updated cached values for', updated, 'cells; header_row in Data=', header_row)\nPY","description":"Populate formulas, compute cached results, and write /root/output/result.xlsx","timeout":120000,"run_in_background":false,"dangerouslyDisableSandbox":false}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f651cb8a-f59e-479d-9e67-807e5db64fe8","uuid":"244bf533-d2d6-46ab-a3e9-d6b568c4e20d"}
